## Review

**8 out of 10, Strong.** Presentation quality, judged with the SlideDeck Co review heuristic (https://slidedeck.co/review-methodology/). The ask, the evidence and the merit of the idea are not rated.

The deck is a clear, well-paced argument: orange dividers (3, 9, 22, 36, 49, 64) mark the sections, and nearly every slide carries one point under a plain headline with a one-line subtitle. Charts are large and uncluttered, with orange used to isolate the subject against grey, as on slide 10 (GAFA against Wintel) and slide 5 (the mobile S-curve passing PCs). The Wintel 1990-2000 versus GAFA 2005-2015 comparison builds in three steps over slides 10 to 12, which is strong teaching sequence. Weaknesses are consistent but not fatal: tiny footers and axis text, a faint logo, sparse quote slides, and a dual-year axis on slide 12 that is awkward to read.

**Worth keeping:** Keep the one-idea-per-slide discipline, the grey-and-orange chart palette, the orange section dividers, and the stepped Wintel versus GAFA build on slides 10 to 12.

**Most useful repair:** Fix the slide 12 axis labels, which read '1990 / 2005' and only three of the eleven bars are labelled, and add a single labelled takeaway on the denser stacked charts such as slides 14 and 15.

**What would change the score:** I would enlarge the small body text on the image-and-text slides (17, 23, 28, 31, 42), replace the very faint footer logo, and add emphasis or visuals to the plain quote slides (8, 16, 26, 59) so the run of text-only pages has more rhythm.

_Confidence high. 72 of 78 pages inspected on contact sheets, 4 at readable size. Reviewed Reviewed 2026-11-08. Model-assisted review (claude-sonnet-5-5)._

## Summary

This 78-slide talk by Benedict Evans, a partner-level analyst, presents his view of the mobile industry, with Andreessen Horowitz branding carried through its charts. It opens on a red title slide, then a bold orange statement, 'Mobile is eating the world.' The next section, 'Mobile deployment,' frames a shift from the creation phase of smartphones to a deployment phase focused on what can be built. The middle sections, 'A new kind of scale' and 'New kinds of computer,' compare the revenue and capex of Google, Apple, Facebook and Amazon with the old Wintel giants, and cover apps, imaging and frictionless computing. Later sections, 'Ecommerce' and 'Cars,' apply the same logic to retail and to electric and autonomous vehicles. The deck closes with a 'Thank you' slide. Each slide carries one idea, short headline-style titles, large simple charts and sourced footers. Section divider slides in a single accent colour pace the argument. Notable slides include the S-curve comparison, the Wintel versus GAFA revenue chart and the machine learning data-versus-rules slide.

## Questions about this deck

**How many slides does the Benedict Evans mobile industry deck have?**

The deck has 78 slides. Slide 1 is the title slide, 'Mobile is eating the world', dated December 2016.

**What does the Benedict Evans deck say about Wintel versus GAFA revenue?**

Slide 10 states that Google, Apple, Facebook & Amazon (GAFA) are 3x the scale of Wintel in its annual revenue ($bn, real terms) chart. Slide 12 adds that GAFA has 10x the scale.

**What does the Benedict Evans deck say about ecommerce and retail?**

Slide 52 says ecommerce is much better at logistics than demand generation. Slide 53 says Amazon is the fourth largest apparel retailer in the USA.

**What error rates does the Benedict Evans deck give for machine learning in image and speech recognition?**

Slide 24 shows image recognition falling from a 28% error rate to 7%. It shows speech recognition falling from a 26% error rate to 4%.

### Slide 5

**Review notes**

- Worth studying: A clean line chart with a restrained grey and orange palette shows the mobile and PC adoption curves against population, with the data doing the explaining.
- Review: Charts are large and uncluttered, with orange used to isolate the subject against grey, as on slide 10 (GAFA against Wintel) and slide 5 (the mobile S-curve passing PCs).

**Text**

The mobile S-Curve is passing the PC S-Curve
Smartphones are moving past the PC and heading for 5bn+ users
Global population (bn)
Mobile users
iOS & Android
smartphones

Adults

ANDREESSEN HOROWITZ
Source: Apple, Google, a16z, TMW, ITU, World Bank

**Layout**

Left-aligned header block with the title at about 19% from the left and the subtitle beneath it, both starting on the same left edge. A full-width light grey chart panel sits in the middle band, with a legend column at the left (about 6% from the left edge) and the plot area to its right. A footer row below the panel holds the page number at the far left, the logo centred-left at about 19%, and the source line right-aligned to about 96% of the width.

**Visuals**

One chart: a stacked-area and line chart titled 'Global population (bn)'. Y-axis: 0 to 6 bn, with labels 0, 1, 2, 3, 4, 5, 6 and dotted horizontal gridlines at each integer. X-axis: years 1985 to 2015, labelled at 1985, 1990, 1995, 2000, 2005, 2010, 2015. No values are printed on the chart, so all values below are estimates. Series: Adults, a grey line (#A6A6A6, about 3 px) rising from about 3.2 bn in 1985 to about 5.35 bn in 2015, passing about 4.0 in 1997 and about 4.5 in 2005. PCs, an orange line (#D07A35, about 3 px) rising from about 0.03 bn in 1985 to about 0.5 bn in 2000 and about 1.55 bn near 2012, then falling slightly to about 1.45 bn in 2015. Mobile users, a light blue-grey filled area (#C9D3E1) rising from about 0 in 1995 to about 0.5 bn in 2000, about 1.0 bn in 2003, about 3.1 bn in 2009, and about 4.8 bn in 2015. iOS & Android smartphones, a mid blue filled area (#708CB0) starting about 2008, rising to about 0.1 bn in 2010, about 0.8 bn in 2012, about 2.05 bn in 2014, and about 2.5 bn in 2015, drawn in front of the Mobile users area. Legend, at the left of the plot: a light blue-grey swatch with 'Mobile users', a mid blue swatch with 'iOS & Android smartphones', an orange line with 'PCs', and a grey line with 'Adults'. No highlighted points. Logo: the Andreessen Horowitz wordmark in grey serif capitals.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF, with a full-width light grey #F2F2F2 band behind the chart. Clean corporate style. White background at the top and in the footer, with a light grey #F2F2F2 band behind the chart. Title in near-black #101010 Proxima Nova Regular, about 5.9% of slide height. Subtitle in black #000000 Proxima Nova Regular, about 3.5% of slide height. Chart title in grey #7F7E7B Proxima Nova Semibold, about 3.5%. Legend and x-axis labels in dark grey #3F3F3F Proxima Nova Regular, about 2.5%. Y-axis labels and source line in grey #7F7E7B, about 2.5%. Accent colours: orange #D07A35 for the PC line, mid blue #708CB0 for iOS & Android, light blue-grey #C9D3E1 for Mobile users, grey #A6A6A6 for Adults. Gridlines are dotted grey. Shapes have square corners, no shadows and no gradients.

### Slide 6

Creation
Deployment
Maturity
Scaling
AR?
Mobile
PCs
Frenzy

5
ANDREESSEN HOROWITZ

**Layout**

Full-width white slide with the title and subtitle left-aligned at about 19% from the left edge in the upper band. Below them a horizontal row of tall grey column panels runs from about 25% to 89% of the height, with a white gutter between panels and small slide number at bottom-left and a serif wordmark beside it at the bottom.

**Visuals**

Diagram: an S-curve drawn as a solid orange line (#CE7C3B, about 0.5% of slide height stroke) starts near the bottom at about x 21.8, y 84.6 with a flat start, bends upward through the Creation panel and ends at a right-pointing orange arrowhead at about x 77.7, y 40.6 in the Deployment/Maturity area. The curve passes through an orange-outlined circle labelled Mobile at centre about x 49.3, y 63.5 (diameter about 10% of width). Labels on the curve: Crazy idea at bottom-left of the curve start, Frenzy above the lower curve, Scaling above the upper curve, Maturity above the arrowhead. Two dotted grey circles (#646464 dotted outline, about 1% stroke): PCs circle centred about x 12.6, y 70.5 and AR? circle centred about x 84.6, y 52.7, each about 10% wide. A dotted grey line (#646464) enters from the lower-left edge and curves up into the PCs circle at about x 0 to 7.8, y 72.9 to 89. A dotted grey arrow leaves the PCs circle at its right side (x 17.5, y 65.8) and points right into the Creation panel, ending with a small filled grey arrowhead at about x 28.6, y 65.5. A dotted grey line runs from the left of the AR circle (about x 70.7, y 53.2) into the circle. A dotted grey line leaves the AR circle upper right (about x 89.4, y 53) and rises to the top-right edge at about x 100, y 26. No photographs, logos other than the wordmark, or icons.

**Design**

Background white #FFFFFF with no gradient. Column panels: pale grey #F2F2F2 for outer panels and mid grey #D9D9D9 for the Creation and Deployment panels, separated by white gutters about 0.8% wide. Title in ProximaNova-Regular (geometric humanist sans-serif, regular weight) colour #101010, about 5.9% of slide height, not bold. Subtitle in ProximaNova-Regular #000000, about 3.5% of slide height. Panel headings Creation and Deployment in ProximaNova-Bold #3F3F3F, about 3.5% of height. Curve labels Maturity, Scaling, Frenzy and Crazy idea in ProximaNova-Regular #3F3F3F, about 3.0% of height. Mobile label in ProximaNova-Bold #3F3F3F, about 3.5%. PCs and AR? labels in ProximaNova-Bold #666666, about 3.5%. Accent colour orange #CE7C3B for the S-curve, its arrowhead and the Mobile circle outline. Grey dotted lines and circles #646464. Slide number in ProximaNova-Regular #666666, about 2.5%. Sharp rectangular panels with no corner radius, no shadows.

### Slide 10

**Review notes**

- Worth studying: The GAFA versus Wintel revenue line chart uses one highlight colour against a neutral grey to make the scale gap immediately visible.
- Review: Charts are large and uncluttered, with orange used to isolate the subject against grey, as on slide 10 (GAFA against Wintel) and slide 5 (the mobile S-curve passing PCs).
- Review: The Wintel 1990-2000 versus GAFA 2005-2015 comparison builds in three steps over slides 10 to 12, which is strong teaching sequence.
- Worth keeping: Keep the one-idea-per-slide discipline, the grey-and-orange chart palette, the orange section dividers, and the stepped Wintel versus GAFA build on slides 10 to 12.

**Text**

Changing of the guard / Google, Apple, Facebook & Amazon (GAFA) are 3x the scale of Wintel / Annual revenue ($bn, real terms) / 500 / Wintel / GAFA / 400 / 300 / 200 / 100 / 0 / 1990 / 1995 / 2000 / 2005 / 2010 / 2015 / 9 / ANDREESSEN HOROWITZ / Source: Bloomberg, a16z

**Layout**

Single-column slide with a full-width white header band holding a left-aligned title and subtitle starting at x 19.1%, and a full-width light-grey chart panel from y 24.7% to 89.1% spanning the slide width. A legend sits at left (x 6.8%) inside the panel, and the line chart plot area runs from about x 15.6% to 96% with the x-axis labels beneath it. The footer at y 92.8% holds the page number at the far left (x 2.5%), a faint wordmark logo at x 19.1%, and the source line right-aligned to x 96%.

**Visuals**

Line chart titled 'Annual revenue ($bn, real terms)' with a y-axis in $bn from 0 to 500 with labelled gridlines at 0, 100, 200, 300, 400 and 500, and an x-axis of years from 1990 to 2015 labelled at 1990, 1995, 2000, 2005, 2010 and 2015. Two series: Wintel as a dark grey line (#666666) and GAFA as an orange line (#D08040 approx.). Values are not printed; approximate values: Wintel about 3 in 1990, 12 in 1995, 42 in 2000, 65 in 2005, 98 in 2010, 149 in 2015; GAFA about 3 in 1990, 9 in 1995, 8 in 2000, 22 in 2005, 121 in 2010, 433 in 2015. Intermediate GAFA peaks about 266 in 2012 and 350 in 2014. The lines cross around 2009 where GAFA is about 80 and Wintel about 82. Legend at left with a short thick line swatch and label: Wintel (grey), GAFA (orange). No highlighted points. Dotted horizontal gridlines in mid grey span the plot width, and a solid grey baseline sits at 0. Footer wordmark 'ANDREESSEN HOROWITZ' in a light grey (#D9D9D9 approx.) serif capital typeface, measured as a 24 by 2 pixel image at x 19.1%, y 92.8%.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF with a light grey #F2F2F2 band behind the chart from y 24.7% to 89.1%. Clean white and light grey corporate style. Background white #FFFFFF for the header and footer and light grey #F2F2F2 for the chart panel. Title in Proxima Nova Regular, near-black #101010, about 5.9% of slide height. Subtitle in Proxima Nova Regular, black #000000, about 3.5% of slide height. Chart title in Proxima Nova Semibold, grey #7F7E7B, about 3.5% of height. Axis and source labels in Proxima Nova Regular at about 2.5% of height, grey #7F7E7B for the y-axis and source, dark grey #3F3F3F for the x-axis and legend labels. Accent colours: orange #D08040 approx. for GAFA and dark grey #666666 for Wintel. Lines are flat with no shadows, gradients or rounded corners; series strokes are about 0.5% of slide height thick; gridlines are fine dotted grey.

### Slide 11

**Review notes**

- Review: The Wintel 1990-2000 versus GAFA 2005-2015 comparison builds in three steps over slides 10 to 12, which is strong teaching sequence.
- Worth keeping: Keep the one-idea-per-slide discipline, the grey-and-orange chart palette, the orange section dividers, and the stepped Wintel versus GAFA build on slides 10 to 12.

**Text**

The Wintel explosion in 1990s – 14x growth / The golden age for Intel and Microsoft saw 14x revenue growth and dominance of tech / Wintel annual revenue ($bn, real terms) / 50 / 40 / 30 / 20 / 10 / 0 / 1990 / 1995 / 2000 / 10 / ANDREESSEN HOROWITZ / Source: Bloomberg, a16z

**Layout**

Single-column slide with a left-aligned title block across the top (title at y 6.9%, subtitle at y 15.7%, both starting at x 19.1%). A full-width light grey chart band spans y 24.7% to 89.1%, with the chart title at x 19.1% and a vertical bar chart spanning x 16.5% to 96.0%. The footer sits below the band at y 92.8%: page number at the left margin (x 2.5%), a faint wordmark centred around x 19.1% to 40.2%, and the source line right-aligned to x 96.0%.

**Visuals**

One vertical bar chart. Title: Wintel annual revenue ($bn, real terms), shown above the plot. Y axis: linear, 0 to 50, gridlines and labels at 0, 10, 20, 30, 40, 50 ($bn), drawn as fine dotted grey lines. X axis: years 1990 to 2000, with labels only at 1990, 1995 and 2000; a solid grey baseline at 0. Series: one series of 11 bars, one per year 1990 to 2000, all in #666666. No values are printed on the bars, so estimated values are: 1990 about 3, 1991 about 4, 1992 about 5, 1993 about 7.5, 1994 about 10, 1995 about 14, 1996 about 19.5, 1997 about 25, 1998 about 29, 1999 about 34.5, 2000 about 41. No legend. No highlighted points. Bars are evenly spaced and roughly 3.5% of slide width each. Also present: a faint wordmark logo reading ANDREESSEN HOROWITZ in light grey serif capitals, with very low contrast against the white footer.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF across the full slide, with a light grey #F2F2F2 band behind the chart area. Clean minimal corporate style. White background (#FFFFFF) with a light grey chart panel (#F2F2F2). Title text in near-black #101010, set in ProximaNova Regular, a geometric sans-serif, at about 5.9% of slide height. Subtitle in black #000000, ProximaNova Regular, about 3.5% of slide height. Chart title in ProximaNova Semibold, mid grey #7F7E7B, about 3.5% of slide height. Axis tick labels in ProximaNova Regular about 2.5% of slide height, mid grey #7F7E7B for the Y axis and dark grey #3F3F3F for the X axis years. Bars solid #666666 with no outline. Gridlines are dotted mid grey. Source and page number in ProximaNova Regular, about 2.5% of height, grey #7F7E7B. No shadows, gradients, rounded corners or borders.

### Slide 12

**Review notes**

- Worth studying: Paired bar charts on the same axes let the reader compare two eras of revenue growth side by side without extra annotation.
- Review: The Wintel 1990-2000 versus GAFA 2005-2015 comparison builds in three steps over slides 10 to 12, which is strong teaching sequence.
- Review: Weaknesses are consistent but not fatal: tiny footers and axis text, a faint logo, sparse quote slides, and a dual-year axis on slide 12 that is awkward to read.
- Worth keeping: Keep the one-idea-per-slide discipline, the grey-and-orange chart palette, the orange section dividers, and the stepped Wintel versus GAFA build on slides 10 to 12.
- Suggested repair: Fix the slide 12 axis labels, which read '1990 / 2005' and only three of the eleven bars are labelled, and add a single labelled takeaway on the denser stacked charts such as slides 14 and 15.

**Text**

**Layout**

Single-column layout on a white slide with the title block left-aligned at x 19.1% and top-aligned at y 6.9%, followed by a subtitle beneath it. A full-width light grey chart band runs from y 24.7% to 89.1%, containing a chart title at the top-left of the band, a legend at the far left, and a vertical bar chart spanning the band. The footer sits on white below the band: page number '11' at the left margin (x 2.5%), a faint wordmark 'ANDREESSEN HOROWITZ' starting at x 19.1%, and the source line right-aligned to x 96%.

**Visuals**

Chart: clustered vertical bar chart, titled 'Annual revenue ($bn, real terms)'. Y-axis: revenue in $bn real terms, range 0 to 500, labelled ticks at 0, 100, 200, 300, 400, 500, with dotted light-grey horizontal gridlines at each labelled tick and a solid grey baseline at 0. X-axis: 11 yearly category pairs; only three are labelled: '1990 / 2005' at the first category, '1995 / 2010' at the sixth, '2000 / 2015' at the last; the unlabelled categories are in between. Series 1, 'Wintel 1990-2000', dark grey #666666 bars, values about: 2, 3, 5, 8, 10, 14, 19, 25, 28, 33, 42. Series 2, 'GAFA 2005-2015', orange #CE7C3B bars, values about: 22, 33, 47, 70, 82, 120, 185, 265, 305, 350, 435. Values are estimates since the slide prints no values. Legend: at far left, dark grey square and label 'Wintel 1990-2000' above an orange square and label 'GAFA 2005-2015'. No highlighted points. Bars are in pairs per year, Wintel bar left of GAFA bar, with no gap between paired bars but a gap between pairs. The wordmark 'ANDREESSEN HOROWITZ' in pale grey serif capitals is a logo at the footer.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF background with a #F2F2F2 light grey band across y 24.7% to 89.1%. Clean minimal corporate style. White background with a #F2F2F2 light grey band behind the chart. Title in ProximaNova Regular, a geometric humanist sans-serif, #101010 near-black, size about 5.9% of slide height. Subtitle in ProximaNova Regular #000000, about 3.5%. Chart title in ProximaNova Semibold #7F7E7B. Axis tick labels ProximaNova Regular #7F7E7B, category labels and legend text #3F3F3F, about 2.5% of slide height. Accent colours: orange #CE7C3B for GAFA series, dark grey #666666 for Wintel series. Gridlines dotted mid-grey, no shadows, no gradients, square bar corners.

**Layout**

Single-column layout with the title and subtitle left-aligned at x 19.1% and sitting in the top quarter of the slide, above a full-width light grey chart panel spanning y 24.7% to 89.1%. The chart fills the panel from the left legend at x 3.3% to the right edge near x 96%. The footer sits at y 92.8% with the page number at the far left (x 2.5%), the logo at x 19.1%, and the source line right-aligned to x 96%.

**Visuals**

Clustered column chart titled 'Top 20 US listed companies by market cap (indexed)'. Y-axis: indexed market cap, 0 to 100, labelled every 10 (0, 10, 20 ... 100), with dotted horizontal gridlines at each 10-unit step. X-axis: two groups labelled 1995 and 2016, with a solid grey baseline at 0. Series colours: GAFA #CE7C3B (orange), Wintel #C9D3E1 (pale blue-grey), All other #666666 (dark grey). Legend at left, top to bottom: GAFA, Wintel, All other. Values are not printed; estimates for 1995 group, left to right, about: All other 100, 96, 86, 85, 70, 68, 53, 50; Wintel about 50; All other about 49; Wintel about 49; All other about 47, 40, 39, 39, 37, 37, 36, 35, 35. Values for 2016 group, left to right, about: GAFA 100, GAFA 87 (the second orange bar), Wintel 70, GAFA 63, GAFA 59, All other 57, 57, 51, 41, 39, 38, 37, 36, 33, 32, 32, 31, 31, 28, then Wintel about 28 at the right end. Highlighted points: the orange GAFA bars in 2016 at the top-left of that group and the Wintel bar at the far right. Gaps separate the 1995 and 2016 groups. Bars are flat-filled with no outlines.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF, with a full-width #F2F2F2 chart panel behind the chart area. Clean corporate style on white. Panel behind chart is #F2F2F2. Title #101010 in Proxima Nova Regular (geometric sans-serif), roughly 5.9% of slide height. Subtitle #000000 Proxima Nova Regular, about 3.5% of slide height. Chart title #7F7E7B Proxima Nova Semibold, about 3.5%. Axis numbers #7F7E7B Proxima Nova Regular, about 2.5%. Year labels and legend text #3F3F3F Proxima Nova Regular, about 2.5%. Gridlines are dotted mid-grey #7F7E7B. Bars are flat with no shadows, gradients or rounded corners. Legend swatches are small squares with square corners.

### Slide 14

**Review notes**

- Suggested repair: Fix the slide 12 axis labels, which read '1990 / 2005' and only three of the eleven bars are labelled, and add a single labelled takeaway on the denser stacked charts such as slides 14 and 15.

**Text**

That scale means much more investment / Annual capex has increased from $1bn to $34bn since 2000 / Annual capex ($bn real) / 35 / 30 / 25 / 20 / 15 / 10 / 5 / 0 / Amazon / Facebook / Apple / Google / Microsoft / 2000 2001 2002 2003 2004 2005 2006 2007 2008 2009 2010 2011 2012 2013 2014 2015 / ANDREESSEN HOROWITZ / Source: Bloomberg, a16z / 13

**Layout**

Two-zone slide. A white header band (top about 25% of the height) holds the left-aligned title and subtitle, both starting at x 19.1%. Below it, a full-width light grey #F2F2F2 chart panel spans y 24.7% to 89.1%. Inside the panel, a legend sits in the left margin (x 4.2%), the chart title sits above the plot at x 19.1%, and the plot spans x about 20.8% to 93.9%. A white footer strip (y about 89% to 100%) holds the page number at left, the a16z wordmark centred-left at x 19.1%, and the source line right-aligned to x 96%.

**Visuals**

Chart: stacked vertical bar chart. Title: Annual capex ($bn real). Y axis: Annual capex in $bn real, linear scale 0 to 35, gridlines and labels every 5 units (0, 5, 10, 15, 20, 25, 30, 35), drawn as light grey dotted horizontal lines. X axis: calendar years 2000 to 2015, one bar per year, with a thin grey baseline at 0. Series stacked bottom to top: Microsoft (#666666), Google (#CE7C3B), Apple (#C9D3E1), Facebook (#50676E), Amazon (#093E58). The slide prints no values for individual segments, so the following are estimates. Totals about: 2000 1; 2001 1.2; 2002 1; 2003 1; 2004 1.6; 2005 1.8; 2006 3.8; 2007 5; 2008 6.4; 2009 5; 2010 8.7; 2011 11.8; 2012 18.4; 2013 24.4; 2014 32.8; 2015 34.2. About 2015 segments: Microsoft about 5.9, Google about 9.9 (top about 15.8), Apple about 11.3 (top about 27.1), Facebook about 2.5 (top about 29.6), Amazon about 4.6 (top about 34.2). About 2000 the bars are all under 1.5 and mostly Microsoft. Legend: vertical list in the left margin, top to bottom Amazon, Facebook, Apple, Google, Microsoft, each with a small filled square swatch (about 1.2% wide) in the series colour followed by the label. No highlighted points. Logo: the wordmark ANDREESSEN HOROWITZ in very light grey, set as a 24 by 2 pixel placed image (wide, thin), rendered in a serif capital face. No other illustrations.

**Design**

Background: Split background: white #FFFFFF overall, with a light grey #F2F2F2 panel across the middle band (y 24.7% to 89.1%). Clean corporate data-visual style. Title in black #101010 ProximaNova Regular sans-serif at about 5.9% of slide height. Subtitle in black #000000 Proxima Nova Regular at about 3.5%. Chart title in grey #7F7E7B Proxima Nova Semibold about 3.5%. Axis numbers in grey #7F7E7B Proxima Nova Regular about 2.5%. Category labels and legend text in dark grey #3F3F3F Proxima Nova Regular about 2.5%. Accent palette: navy #093E58, slate #50676E, pale blue-grey #C9D3E1, orange #CE7C3B, dark grey #666666. Gridlines are dotted mid grey. Bars are flat with square corners, no shadows, no gradients, no outlines. Page number #666666 and source line #7F7E7B, both about 2.5%.

### Slide 15

**Review notes**

- Suggested repair: Fix the slide 12 axis labels, which read '1990 / 2005' and only three of the eleven bars are labelled, and add a single labelled takeaway on the denser stacked charts such as slides 14 and 15.

**Text**

And 10x more people
(Though this chart includes a lot of warehouse and retail staff)
Permanent employees (000)
Amazon
Facebook
Apple
Google
Microsoft

500
400

200
100

2000 2001 2002 2003 2004 2005 2006 2007 2008 2009 2010 2011 2012 2013 2014 2015

ANDREESSEN HOROWITZ
Source: Bloomberg

**Layout**

White header band above a full-width light grey chart panel; the title and subtitle are left-aligned at about 19% of slide width, not on the left margin. The legend sits at the far left of the grey panel, with the chart's y-axis labels and plot to its right. A white footer row holds the page number at far left, the faint Andreessen Horowitz wordmark at about 19% x, and the source line flush right at about 96% x.

**Visuals**

Stacked column chart. Title: 'Permanent employees (000)' in semibold grey text above the plot. Y axis: 0 to 600 (thousands of permanent employees), labelled 0, 100, 200, 300, 400, 500, 600, with dotted horizontal gridlines at each 100 step. X axis: years 2000 to 2015, one column per year. Each column is stacked bottom to top: Microsoft (#666666), Google (#CE7C3B), Apple (#C9D3E1), Facebook (#50676E), Amazon (#093E58). Legend at left, top to bottom: Amazon, Facebook, Apple, Google, Microsoft, each with a small square swatch. Values are not printed on the slide, so these are estimates: column totals about 57 (2000), 64 (2001), 70 (2002), 78 (2003), 81 (2004), 87 (2005), 114 (2006), 134 (2007), 162 (2008), 172 (2009), 197 (2010), 243 (2011), 313 (2012), 352 (2013), 437 (2014), 531 (2015). Microsoft segment about 40 (2000), 47 (2001), 51 (2002), 55 (2003), 57 (2004), 61 (2005), 71 (2006), 78 (2007), 91 (2008), 94 (2009), 88 (2010), 92 (2011), 95 (2012), 98 (2013), 128 (2014), 118 (2015). Columns are narrow, roughly 40% of each category width, with no outlines. No highlighted points. The 2015 Amazon segment is the tallest single segment, about 232. Small logo: Andreessen Horowitz wordmark in light grey serif capitals at the footer.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF across the top and footer, with a light grey #F2F2F2 panel behind the chart.

**Layout**

Two-column split: a white left column (0 to 35.1% of width) holds the title at top-left and the body text below it, while a light grey right panel (35.1 to 100% of width) holds one framed photograph vertically centred in its upper and middle area. The page number sits bottom-left at 2.5% from the left, the faint Andreessen Horowitz wordmark sits bottom-centre of the left column, and a grey caption runs along the bottom of the right panel starting at about 36.8%, all on one baseline near 93% of height.

**Visuals**

One photograph: exterior of an Apple retail store at dusk, shown as a wide 768 by 512 pixel image. Subject: a multi-storey glass-and-steel building with a glass facade lit from inside, a white Apple logo glowing on the glass at centre, a broad elevated canopy of slanted dark panels along the roof, and an overhead glass walkway spanning a city street. A row of cars with red tail-light streaks (motion blur) travels on the road in the foreground right, with yellow painted road markings at the bottom; a tree stands at left foreground and skyscrapers rise behind. Composition: eye-level wide view, the building centred and filling most of the frame, not cropped, shown as a rectangular boxed image with square corners and no overlay or tint. Lighting: blue twilight sky with warm white and amber interior lighting from the store. Dominant colours: deep blue, slate grey steel, warm white, amber and red accents. Second visual: the Andreessen Horowitz wordmark in capital serif letters, very light grey, sitting in the bottom-centre of the left column; it is a logo, not a photograph.

**Design**

Background: Split background: solid white #FFFFFF on the left, with a solid light grey #F2F2F2 panel covering the right 64.9% of the slide. Title typeface is Proxima Nova Regular, a geometric sans-serif, at about 5.9% of slide height, colour near-black #101010, two lines, left-aligned. Caption is Proxima Nova Regular at about 2.5%, colour #7F7E7B. The Andreessen Horowitz wordmark is light grey, about #D9D9D9, in a serif capital face. The photograph has square corners and no border. No accent colours are used in the typography.

### Slide 23

**Text**

“Is there a dog in / this picture?”
After 50 years of work, computer vision / systems got this right 72% of the time. / A whole class of similar problems – / easy for people and hard/impossible for / computers. / Consensus: decades more work. / Then, in 2012, machine learning.
22
ANDREESSEN HOROWITZ
Source: Imagenet

**Layout**

Two-column split: a white left panel covering about 35% of the width holds the title, body text and footer, and a light grey right panel (#F2F2F2) covers the remaining 65%. The title sits top-left at about 6.9% from the top with a left margin of about 3.1%. The body text is left-aligned in the lower-middle of the left column, and the footer row sits at about 92.8% from the top, with the slide number at the far left and the source line at the bottom right of the grey panel. The photo grid is centred in the grey panel.

**Visuals**

One photograph: a square 4 by 4 collage of 16 abutting tiles, with no gaps and no frame, placed at x 45.9%, y 12.0%, w 43.8%, h 75.9% (780 by 760 pixels). Each tile is a close-up photo of either a chihuahua dog face or a blueberry or chocolate chip muffin. Row 1 from left: blueberry muffin, tan chihuahua face in profile looking left, blueberry muffin in a paper cup, cream-coloured chihuahua puppy looking forward. Row 2: tan chihuahua face looking forward, chocolate chip muffin, tan chihuahua face with wide eyes, blueberry muffin with blueberries. Row 3: chocolate chip muffin with a hole in the centre, tan chihuahua face looking forward, blueberry muffin with two blue-purple blueberries, chihuahua with a pink top and open mouth. Row 4: tan chihuahua face close to camera, a group of blueberry muffins on a plate, two small chihuahua puppies together, chocolate chip muffin with yellow paper cup. Lighting is even and natural, with warm tan, golden and purple-blue dominant colours. The grid is full-square, not cropped at the outer edges. No overlay or tint.

**Design**

Background: white left panel (#FFFFFF) and light grey right panel (#F2F2F2) with no gradient. Title text colour #101010, set in ProximaNova Regular (geometric sans-serif, regular weight), about 5.9% of slide height, lowercase with an opening curly quote. Body text colour #000000, ProximaNova Regular, about 3.0% of slide height, with generous line spacing and blank lines between paragraphs. Footer slide number colour #666666, ProximaNova Regular, about 2.5% of slide height. Source line colour #7F7E7B, ProximaNova Regular, about 2.5%. Logo wordmark in very pale grey (about #D0D0D0), serif capitals. No shapes, lines, shadows or corner radii; the photo grid has square corners.

### Slide 25

**Review notes**

- Worth studying: A two-column old-versus-new comparison reduces the machine learning shift to a simple contrast that is easy to remember.

**Text**

Machine learning: data instead of rules
Fundamental shift in AI from trying to codify perception to massive data and applied maths
Old: rules
Machine learning: data
Build systems to look for ears,
noses, legs, fur…
Hire linguists and write grammar
rules.
Try to codify how human
intelligence works (though we don’t
really know).
10k pictures labelled ‘dog’ and 10k
labelled ‘no dog’, and let a neural
network work it out.
Learning rather than rules.
Possible now because we have
~1m times more computing & data.

ANDREESSEN HOROWITZ

Alpha Go – board / games and air-con / Google took the machine learning / platform that won Go and applied it to / data center cooling. / Achieved a 15% energy saving. / Building systems that find patterns in / data. / 27 / ANDREESSEN HOROWITZ

**Layout**

Two-column split: a light grey text panel fills the left 35 percent of the slide, and a full-height photograph fills the right side. The title sits top left at about 7 percent from the top, with the body text left-aligned below it at about 36 to 64 percent height. The page number sits bottom left at about 93 percent height, and a faint wordmark sits bottom centre-left on the same baseline, with left margin about 3 percent.

**Visuals**

One photograph of an indoor data-centre cooling plant. Subject: a dense network of large colourful painted pipes and ducts, with yellow, red, blue and green pipes running overhead and down to the floor, flanged valves with handwheels, and suspended chains. Below are light blue plate heat exchangers, stainless steel tanks, and a red pump casing on a steel frame with yellow and black hazard-striped base plates. A red fire extinguisher stands on the floor, and a green mat lies at the bottom left. Composition: wide-angle, looking up and across the ceiling, with the pipe run receding to the right. Lighting: overhead fluorescent tubes and small spotlights, neutral and even. Dominant colours: saturated blue, yellow, red and green, with grey steel and a pale grey concrete floor. The photo is full-height and bleeds to the top, bottom and right edges. Its left part sits under the grey panel, so only the right part is visible. No overlay or tint. Also a faint grey wordmark, ANDREESSEN HOROWITZ, in a thin serif capital typeface, low contrast, centred on about x 11 to 32 percent.

**Design**

Background is white #FFFFFF with a left panel of light grey #F2F2F2 covering the left 35 percent. Title colour is near-black #101010. Body text is black #000000. Page number is grey #666666. The wordmark is light grey, about #BFBFBF. Title font: ProximaNova-Regular, a geometric sans-serif, regular weight, sentence-style lowercase and capitals, with the title at about 5.9 percent of slide height. Body font: ProximaNova-Regular, sans-serif, regular weight, about 3.0 percent of slide height, with about 4 percent spacing between paragraphs. Page number is ProximaNova-Regular at about 2.5 percent. No rules, shapes, borders, shadows, gradients or rounded corners.

ANDREESSEN HOROWITZ
33

**Layout**

Single-slide content area with left-aligned title and subtitle starting at about 19% of slide width, sitting in the top band. Two equal circles sit side by side in the lower two-thirds, centred horizontally in the left and right halves, with a double-headed dotted arrow bridging the gap between them. The page number sits bottom-left at about 2.5% margin, and a light grey wordmark sits bottom-centre-left aligned with the title's left edge.

**Visuals**

Two outlined circles of equal size and a double-headed dotted arrow. Left circle: orange outline (about #CC7A38), roughly 2.5 px at slide scale, no fill, containing the heading Surge in complexity and three text blocks. Right circle: pale blue-grey outline (about #C8D1DD), same size and stroke weight, no fill, containing the heading Rush to abstraction and two text blocks. Arrow: dotted dark grey line (about #666666) with filled triangular arrowheads at both ends, running horizontally between the right edge of the left circle and the left edge of the right circle at about the vertical centre of the circles. Not a chart, table, photograph or logo beyond the wordmark.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF. White background (#FFFFFF) with no gradients, shadows or textures. Titles and headings are near-black (#101010 for the title, #000000 for the headings and body). Accents are the orange circle outline (about #CC7A38), the pale blue-grey circle outline (about #C8D1DD), and the dark grey dotted arrow (about #666666). Typeface is Proxima Nova, a geometric sans-serif: Regular for title, subtitle and body; Bold for the two circle headings. Title is about 5.9% of slide height; subtitle and body about 3.5%; headings about 4.0%; page number about 2.5%. Circle outlines are thin, with no corner radii (true circles), and the arrow is a dotted stroke with triangular heads.

**Layout**

Left-aligned title and subtitle sit in the upper area with the title starting at about 19% from the left edge, and a row of vertical columns fills the middle band from about 25% to 88% of the height. The columns run edge to edge with thin white gutters, and the page number sits bottom-left at about 2.5% from the left edge, with the small Andreessen Horowitz wordmark centred near 19% to 40% at the bottom.

**Visuals**

One line-and-circle diagram over a column background. A single orange (#CE7C3B) S-curve, about 4 px wide, runs from a plain start point at about x 22%, y 85% rising to the right through a circle at about x 31% to 41%, y 71% to 89%, then curves steeply up through the gap between the Creation and Deployment columns and flattens toward the right, ending in a small filled arrowhead pointing right at about x 78%, y 41%. The curve is labelled from left to right and bottom to top with 'Crazy idea' (start), 'Machine Learning' (inside the circle), 'Frenzy' (on the steep rise), 'Scaling' (on the upper flattening section) and 'Maturity' (at the arrow end). The circle has an orange outline of about 0.4% of slide height and a light grey #D9D9D9 fill, with bold dark grey text centred inside. Column backgrounds: light grey #F2F2F2 bands at about x 2.6% to 25.4% and x 73.4% to 96.2% with a sliver at the far right edge; mid grey #D9D9D9 columns at about x 26.1% to 49.0% (Creation) and x 49.8% to 72.6% (Deployment), separated by white gutters of about 0.8% width. The wordmark 'ANDREESEN HOROWITZ' is set in a thin serif capital typeface in pale grey, about 2.1% of slide height tall.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF. Plain white background with flat grey columns and no shadows, gradients or rounded corners. Title colour #101010, subtitle colour #000000, column headings and circle text #3F3F3F, curve and circle outline #CE7C3B, page number #666666, wordmark pale grey approximately #CCCCCC. Typeface is Proxima Nova (geometric sans-serif); title is Regular at about 5.9% of slide height, subtitle Regular at about 3.5%, column headings and 'Machine Learning' Bold at about 3.5%, other labels Regular at about 3.0%, page number Regular at about 2.5%. Title and labels are left-aligned, sentence case, no letter-spacing changes. Columns have square corners, the curve is a smooth round-joined stroke.

**Design**

### Slide 39

**Text**

More important: a new generation of computing

Intensely personal
Always with you
Imaging as primary input
Touch, tilt, location, microphone
App stores
Ignore battery & bandwidth
1000x faster
Cloud & machine learning
And yet much easier to use…
38

**Layout**

Two-zone layout on a white slide. The title and subtitle are left-aligned at x 19.1% and sit in the top band, above a light grey panel (y 24.7% to 89.0%, full width). The left side of the panel holds a photograph of a beige vintage computer (x 2.8% to 43.1%). The right side holds a 3 by 3 grid of tiles (x 48.4% to 98.8%, y 25.7% to 87.6%). The page number sits bottom-left at x 2.5%, y 92.8%, and the wordmark sits beside it at x 19.1%, y 92.5%, both on the white strip below the panel. Margins are about 2.5% at the left and right edges.

**Visuals**

A photograph of a beige Macintosh-style desktop computer shown front-on: a boxed monitor with a black screen, a floppy disk slot below the screen, a small rainbow Apple logo at lower left of the case, a beige keyboard in front, and a small mouse at right. Lighting is flat and even, with soft shadows under the keyboard. Dominant colours are beige #D8D0BE and black #1A1A1A on a near-white ground. The image is not cropped, is not full-bleed, and has no border or overlay. A solid grey #A3A3A3 right-pointing block arrow runs horizontally from the computer's right edge (about x 30.4% to 46.0%, y 50.0% to 56.7%) toward the tile grid, with its shaft at mid-height and a triangular head at the right end. A tiny 24 by 2 pixel mark sits at x 19.1%, y 92.8%, nearly invisible. The grid of nine white rectangles is described under elements.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF, with a #F2F2F2 light grey panel across y 24.7% to 89.0%. White background with a #F2F2F2 light grey panel behind the photo and tiles. Title colour #101010, subtitle colour #000000, tile text colour #3F3F3F, page number colour #666666, arrow colour #A3A3A3. Title is Proxima Nova Regular (geometric sans-serif) at about 5.9% of slide height. Subtitle is Proxima Nova Regular at about 3.5%. Tile text is Proxima Nova Light (sans-serif) at about 3.95%. Page number is Proxima Nova Regular at about 2.5%. Tiles are flat white rectangles with square corners and no shadows, separated by thin grey gutters. The bottom-right tile has no white fill and blends into the #F2F2F2 panel. The wordmark is a pale grey (#D0D0D0-ish) high-contrast serif capital set with wide letter-spacing.

**Layout**

Three equal dark-grey, orange and mid-grey circles sit in a row across the middle of a light-grey band, joined by two grey block arrows, with the title and subtitle left-aligned at x 19.1% above the band. The page number sits bottom-left at x 2.5% and the wordmark bottom-left of centre at x 19.1%, both on the white footer strip below the band.

**Visuals**

Three filled circles, each about 20.4% of slide width by 36.2% of slide height, so they are true circles. Circle 1 at x 19.1%, y 38.8%, fill #3F3F3F (dark charcoal), contains the white text 'Both more sophisticated and easier to use'. Circle 2 at x 47.9%, y 38.8%, fill #CE7B3B (burnt orange), contains the white text 'New sensors, inputs and interfaces, machine learning'. Circle 3 at x 76.6%, y 38.8%, fill #7F7F7F (mid grey), contains the white text 'New creation: live, social, broadcast, touch, imaging'. Between circles 1 and 2 is a right-pointing block arrow at x 41.6%, y 52.3%, 4.4% by 9.3%, fill #A3A3A3, flat with no outline; a second identical arrow at x 70.3%, y 52.3% sits between circles 2 and 3. The arrows point left to right, showing the sequence circle 1 to circle 2 to circle 3. A small wordmark 'ANDREESSEN HOROWITZ' in light-grey serif capitals (raster image, 24 by 2 pixels) sits at x 19.1%, y 92.8%.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF across the full slide, with a light-grey #F2F2F2 band from y 24.7% to 89.0%. Plain flat design on white with a light-grey #F2F2F2 band behind the diagram. Titles are set in ProximaNova-Regular, a geometric sans-serif, in near-black #101010. Subtitle and body text are near-black #000000 ProximaNova-Regular. Circle text is ProximaNova-Light, white #FFFFFF, centred. Title cap height is about 5.9% of slide height, subtitle about 3.5%, circle text about 3.95%, page number about 2.5%. Accents are #3F3F3F, #CE7B3B, #7F7F7F and the arrows #A3A3A3. Lines and shapes have no outlines, shadows or gradients; circles are perfectly round with no corner radius.

### Slide 42

**Text**

How many old assumptions does live video break? / Assume high quality camera, fast CPU/GPU for encoding and effects, touch, always on, always in your pocket. / Assume unlimited bandwidth & battery. / Assume 1bn high-end smartphones on earth - there can be 50 companies trying this. / And turn the whole phone into the camera. / 41 / ANDREESSEN HOROWITZ / Source: Houseparty, Yixia

**Layout**

Two-column split slide. The left white column (about 0 to 35% of slide width) holds the title at top, a body text block below it, and the page number and logo along the bottom margin. The right light-grey panel (about 35 to 100% width) holds two phone mockups side by side, centred in the panel, with the source line at bottom right. Text is left-aligned at about 3% from the left edge. The title sits top-left at about 7% from the top; the body block starts at about 36.5%. The slide number 41 sits bottom-left at about 93% from the top, and the Andreessen Horowitz wordmark sits bottom-centre of the left column.

**Visuals**

Two smartphone mockups in dark grey/black frames, shown upright and side by side, with no overlap. Left phone: a Houseparty video call screen showing a 2x3 grid of live video tiles of smiling young people (top-left a woman with long brown hair, top-right a young man in sunglasses and checked shirt, middle-left a partly visible woman against a grey street, middle-right a laughing woman with blonde hair, bottom-left partly visible, bottom-right a smiling woman with long dark hair). A white rounded notification banner at the top of the screen reads 'Sara Tabb is the house!' with a small house icon. Right phone: a Yixia live-streaming screen with a full-frame photo of a young woman with bangs wearing a cream sweater with black patterned knit, arms raised behind her head, with a gold crown graphic with a pink heart and the word 'Princess' in pink-and-gold lettering. Chinese-language overlay text and chat bubbles sit over the lower half of the photo, with a heart-shaped gift icon and pink floating hearts on the right side; a row of small circular avatars sits above a bottom input bar reading 说点什么吧. Top-left of the screen has a username card 杉树卤虾 with viewer count text and a pill button 守护排行榜. Lighting is flat and even, the photo is warm cream and brown with pink accents. The phone screens are not cropped and are boxed by their device frames; the left phone is a rendered device with a gradient-free black body.

**Design**

Background: Split background: white #FFFFFF on the left and light grey #F2F2F2 on the right panel. Clean minimal look. Left column pure white, right panel light grey #F2F2F2. Title in regular-weight geometric sans-serif (Proxima Nova style), near-black #101010, roughly 5.9% of slide height per line, with about 1.2 line spacing. Body text regular-weight Proxima Nova style in black #000000 at about 3.0% of slide height, line spacing about 1.35, with paragraphs separated by one blank line. Page number and source line in grey #666666 and #7F7E7B at about 2.5% of slide height. Logo wordmark in pale grey serif capitals. No accent colours, no shadows on the slide furniture; phone mockups have dark rounded device frames.

### Slide 43

**Text**

Frictionless
computing
No buttons, apps or intermediate steps,
no ‘computer stuff’ between you and the
service – just use it (hopefully).
Hardware sensors as unbundled apps -
deep links within apps.
Apps moved to a new context.
And of course all built with commodity
components from the smartphone
supply chain.
42
ANDREESSEN HOROWITZ

**Layout**

Two-column split slide. A full-height light grey panel occupies the left 35.1% of the width and holds the title at the top and the body text below it, left-aligned with a left margin of about 3.1%. The right area is white and holds two product photographs placed side by side at different heights, with the Amazon Echo on the left-centre and the glasses on the right. The page number 42 sits bottom-left at about 2.5% and the light grey firm wordmark sits bottom-centre of the grey panel.

**Visuals**

Two product photographs on a white background, no captions. (1) Amazon Echo smart speaker: a tall black cylinder, roughly three times as tall as wide, photographed straight-on in a studio setting with soft even lighting and no visible cast shadow. The top cap carries a thin blue light ring and small touch controls (microphone and action buttons). The lower half has a dense perforated black speaker mesh, and the base has the word 'amazon' in small light grey lettering with a smile arrow. The image is not cropped and sits inside a plain white field. (2) Eyewear with cameras, the Snapchat Spectacles style: a turquoise-teal frame with round mirrored light blue lenses, a bridge and a thin top bar, photographed front-on with the arms folded out of view, lit evenly with soft highlights on the lenses. Each outer corner has a small dark camera lens with a yellow ring. Dominant colours are teal #1FB5C4 and light blue #8FD3E8 on the lenses. The image is not cropped and is not boxed. Several other embedded image objects on the slide are 1 pixel spacers and are invisible.

**Design**

Background: Split background: white #FFFFFF across the slide with a light grey #F2F2F2 panel covering the left 35.1%. Clean, minimal, sans-serif presentation style. Background is white with a light grey #F2F2F2 left panel covering about 35% of the width, with no gradients, shadows or corner radii. Title is in ProximaNova Regular at about 5.9% of slide height, colour #101010, sentence case, left aligned, two lines with tight leading. Body text is ProximaNova Regular at about 3.0% of slide height, colour #000000, left aligned, roughly 1.7 line spacing, paragraphs separated by blank lines, with 'use' in italic. ### Slide 46

**Text**

Changing friction and changing choices
Massive proliferation of UX models. All about removing friction. And choices…
Removing questions and friction
But also choices
Do you have the printer driver?
Questions are also choices
What’s your password?
Where did you save that file?
Huge strategic incentive for big
platforms to be the ones to answer
…
the question for you
What soap do you want to buy?
With which app?
“Who owns the customer?”
45
ANDREESSEN HOROWITZ

**Layout**

Three-band layout on a white slide: a centred-left title block across the top, two column headers at about 27.5% of the height, and a two-column list area in a light grey panel spanning the middle band (about 35.6% to 89.1% of the height). A white chevron stripe runs vertically from top to bottom of the grey panel at about 53% of the width, splitting the left column (questions, left-aligned at x 7.6%) from the right column (answers, left-aligned at x 61.7%). Footer band below the panel holds the page number at bottom-left (x 2.5%) and a light grey Andreessen Horowitz wordmark starting at x 19.1%. The title is left-aligned at x 19.1%, with left margin about 7.6% for content.

**Visuals**

A chevron divider running the full height of the grey panel: a white stripe about 3% of the width wide, pointing right, with its apex near x 52.5% and y 62%, bordered on its right edge by a thin mid-grey (#BFBFBF) band that forms a shadow-like edge. Left of the chevron is a flat light grey (#F2F2F2) panel; right of it is another flat light grey panel of the same tone. No photographs, charts, icons or diagrams other than the wordmark logo.

**Design**

Background is white (#FFFFFF) for the top and footer bands, with a flat light grey (#F2F2F2) panel behind the list area. Title text is near-black #101010 in ProximaNova Regular (geometric sans-serif), about 5.9% of slide height in size. Subtitle is black #000000 ProximaNova Regular at about 3.5% of slide height. Column headers are dark grey #474747 ProximaNova Bold at about 3.95% of slide height. List body text is dark grey #3F3F3F to #474747 ProximaNova Light at about 3.95% of slide height, with generous spacing between lines. Accent: chevron stripe white with a #BFBFBF edge. Page number is #666666 ProximaNova Regular at about 2.5%. Logo wordmark is pale grey, serif capitals with wide letter-spacing. No shadows, gradients, rounded corners or line borders apart from the chevron edge.

### Slide 47

**Text**

From direct, physical interaction with data…
46
ANDREESSEN HOROWITZ
Source: IBM

**Layout**

A single centred title sits in the top band on a white field, with a full-width photograph filling the lower part of the slide from just below the title to the bottom edge. A semi-transparent white footer band runs across the bottom 11% of the slide, holding the page number at the left, a faint logo in the centre-left and the source line at the right. Left margin for the page number is about 2.5% and right margin for the source is about 4%.

**Visuals**

One photograph: a black-and-white photograph of two hands interacting with a light grey rectangular block with raised dot patterns resembling Braille. A hand with fingers spread presses on the upper part of the block from the top-left, and a second hand approaches from the right edge with the index finger extended toward the block. The block occupies the centre-left of the frame, with its face covered in small dark raised dots in irregular clusters. The composition is a wide landscape crop, full-bleed across the slide width, running from about 12% to the bottom edge and partly covered by the footer band. Lighting is soft and directional from the upper left, with a dark background at the top right. Dominant colours are greys: near-black background, mid-grey skin tones and light grey block (#D9D9D9 approximately). The bottom of the photograph shows a faint ruler-like object partly under the footer band. No overlay on the photo apart from the footer band.

**Design**

Background: White #FFFFFF with a full-width black-and-white photograph beneath the title area. White background with a white title area. Title in Proxima Nova Regular, sentence case, dark near-black text #101010, centred, roughly 5.9% of slide height. Page number and source line in Proxima Nova Regular in grey tones (#666666 and #7F7E7B). Footer band is white at 60% and 80% opacity layered over the bottom of the photograph, softening it. Logo is set in a light grey (about #BFBFBF after overlay) spaced serif capitals. No shapes, borders, corner radii, shadows or gradients.

Margins are about 2.5% on the left and about 4% on the right.

**Visuals**

Photograph (full-bleed, spanning the full slide width, visible from about 24.7% of the height down to the footer). Subject: an animated 3D character, a young woman with a brown ponytail, brown jacket with a pink emblem, dark trousers and white boots, standing on a white table at the left and pointing a wooden wand with a glowing tip toward a wall. On the wall, projected blue-and-dark numerals float in mirrored-looking form: a vertical fraction showing '+29' over '107', a row reading '72 80 87 91 107' with blue arcs (jumps) drawn above them, and '10 10 1 8' above the row, with the number 107 circled in red. At right, a real child (a girl with dark curly hair, looking left, denim shirt with a white arrow print over a grey t-shirt with a red collar) holds a pencil and a sheet of paper. Composition: wide horizontal frame, the animated figure at left third, the child at right, the wall with numbers in the centre. Lighting: soft natural indoor daylight. Dominant colours: warm beige, grey-white wall, denim blue, dark brown wood chair, pale table. The photo is not boxed and has no tint, but the bottom strip is overlaid by white at 60% and 80% opacity, which fades the lower part and the wordmark. A thin strip of table and paper is visible behind the footer. No charts, tables or icons. Logo: the Andreessen Horowitz wordmark in light grey serif capitals, faded by the white overlay.

**Design**

Background: White #FFFFFF, with the photograph covering the lower area behind a translucent white footer band. White background (#FFFFFF) with a photograph filling the lower part. Title in a clean geometric sans-serif (ProximaNova-Regular style), regular weight, about 5.9% of slide height, colour near-black #101010. Footer text and page number in the same family at about 2.5% of slide height, grey #666666 and #7F7E7B. Footer band is white at 60% and 80% opacity over the photo. No shapes, borders, corner radii, shadows or gradients.

### Slide 50

**Text**

Retailers as newspapers
“History doesn’t repeat, but sometimes it rhymes”
Fixed cost base with falling revenue.
Shift from paid to freemium, unbundling, different skills.
The distribution advantage moves.
New medium means different consumption – you buy different things, not just in different places.
ANDREESSEN HOROWITZ
49

**Layout**

Single-column title block with the title and quotation subtitle left-aligned at about x 19.1%, starting near y 6.9%. Below, a full-width light grey band (y 24.7% to 89.0%) holds a two-column composition: a newspaper stack image on the left (x 9.5% to 39.1%) with a grey arrow pointing right into a left-aligned text column starting at x 48.1%. The page number sits bottom-left at x 2.5%, y 92.8%, and the wordmark is bottom-left at x 19.1%, aligned with the title edge.

**Visuals**

One photograph and one arrow. Photograph: a cut-out, greyscale stack of folded newspapers, roughly square-pyramidal, with the top sheet showing a printed page of grey newsprint columns and tables; the stack is seen from slightly above, centred in its box, with a white background cut out around it and a soft natural light from the upper left, no shadow. The stack is not cropped, boxed or tinted; its dominant colours are light grey, off-white and charcoal. Arrow: a solid right-pointing block arrow, flat fill #A3A3A3, shaft about 3% of slide height thick with a triangular head, its left end tucked behind the right edge of the newspaper stack, pointing at the body text. Also a small wordmark logo (see elements) in light grey serif capitals.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF, with a full-width band of #F2F2F2 from about 24.7% to 89% of slide height. Clean minimal corporate look on a white slide. Background white #FFFFFF with a light grey band #F2F2F2 across the middle. Title in ProximaNova Regular (geometric humanist sans-serif), near-black #101010, about 5.9% of slide height. Subtitle in ProximaNova Regular, black #000000, about 3.5% of slide height. Body text in ProximaNova Light, dark grey #3F3F3F, about 4.4% of slide height, with generous line spacing and blank lines between points. Accent is the grey arrow #A3A3A3. Page number ProximaNova Regular #666666 about 2.5%. Wordmark in a light grey (about #D0D0D0) serif capital typeface. No borders, shadows, gradients or rounded corners.

### Slide 51

**Text**

Everything the internet did to media will / happen to retail / Break up old / bundles and / aggregators / New / aggregators / emerge / And they shape / consumption in / different ways / 50 / ANDREESSEN HOROWITZ

**Layout**

Three large circles sit in a horizontal row across the middle of a light grey band, joined by two block arrows. The title is top-left, left-aligned at about x 19 percent, in the white area above the band. Single-column slide with a title block at the top, starting at x 19.1 percent and left-aligned with the subtitle and the chart heading. Line chart titled 'Ecommerce as % US retail revenue*'. X-axis: years 1998 to 2015, one label per year, no axis title. Y-axis: 0% to 14% in 2% steps, labels at left, no axis title. Footnote explains exclusions. Small faint wordmark ANDREESSEN HOROWITZ in the footer, rendered as a light grey logo image about 21 percent wide.

### Slide 54

**Text**

But how do you know what to buy?
Amazon is Google for products, but we don’t
have a Facebook or Buzzfeed – we don’t
have suggestion and discovery.
The internet lets you buy, but it doesn’t let
you shop.
53
ANDREESSEN HOROWITZ

**Layout**

Single left-aligned text block with a ragged right edge, starting at about 19.5% from the left and spanning roughly 25% to 71% of the slide height, so it sits in the vertical middle and right-of-centre. Three paragraphs separated by blank line gaps. No title, header or graphic. Page number sits in the bottom-left margin at about 2.5% from the left, and a faint wordmark sits at the bottom, starting at about 19% from the left, on the same baseline band (about 92.8% to 95% of the height).

**Visuals**

none apart from the faint Andreessen Horowitz wordmark in the bottom band. It is a logo: the wordmark ANDREESSEN HOROWITZ set in a light, high-contrast serif in capitals, in very pale grey that nearly merges with the background. It is not a photograph, chart or diagram.

**Design**

Background: Solid flat colour #F2F2F2 (light grey) covering the whole slide. Flat light-grey background with no gradient, texture or shadow. Body text is a light geometric sans-serif (Proxima Nova Light) in dark grey #3F3F3F, sentence case, regular letter-spacing, left aligned, with no bullets. Body text is about 5.4% of slide height, all one size, with about 1.5 line spacing within paragraphs and about one line of space between paragraphs. Page number is Proxima Nova Regular in mid grey #666666 at about 2.5% of slide height. The wordmark is a pale grey serif. No accent colour, rules or rounded shapes.

### Slide 55

**Text**

Digital demand generation, step 1: spend $1tr / $500bn spent annually on ads (a third on digital) – and another $500bn on marketing / Global ad spending ($bn, real terms) / 600 / Other internet / Google / 500 / Print / Other / 400 / TV / 300 / 200 / 100 / 0 / 1980 / 1985 / 1990 / 1995 / 2000 / 2005 / 2010 / 2015 / 54 / ANDREESSEN HOROWITZ / Source: Zenith, Google, a16z

**Layout**

Single column on a white slide with a left margin of about 2.5 percent. The title sits top-left at about 6.9 percent from the top, left-aligned at x 19.1 percent, with a one-line subtitle beneath it at the same left edge. A full-width light grey chart panel spans from about 24.7 to 89.1 percent of the height, containing the chart title, a legend column at the far left and the stacked area plot aligned to x 19.4 percent. The footer row at about 92.8 percent holds the page number at the far left, a faint publisher wordmark at x 19.1 percent and the source line right-aligned to about 95.9 percent.

**Visuals**

One stacked area chart of global ad spending, 1980 to 2015, annual data, in US dollars billions in real terms. Y axis: linear, $bn, from 0 to 600, labelled every 100 with dotted horizontal gridlines at 100, 200, 300, 400, 500 and 600. X axis: years labelled 1980, 1985, 1990, 1995, 2000, 2005, 2010 and 2015. No values are printed on the series, so all values are estimates marked about. Series stacked bottom to top: TV (#666666), about 24 in 1980 rising to about 186 in 2015; Other (#C9D3E1), top of the band about 70 above TV in 2015 (top about 256); Print (#50676E), top about 354 in 2015; Google (#CE7C3B), top about 422 in 2015; Other internet (#D89562), top about 508 in 2015, the highest total on the chart. Other internet and Google are near zero before about 1995 and grow steeply after 2005. The 2008 to 2009 dip is visible in all series. Legend at far left, top to bottom: Other internet (#D89562), Google (#CE7C3B), Print (#50676E), Other (#C9D3E1), TV (#666666), each a small filled square followed by its label. No highlighted points. Logo: faint grey serif capitals reading ANDREESSEN HOROWITZ in the footer.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF across the full slide, with a light grey #F2F2F2 band behind the chart. Clean flat corporate style. Background white with a light grey #F2F2F2 chart panel. Title in regular sans-serif (Proxima Nova-like) in near-black #101010, roughly 5.9 percent of slide height. Subtitle in regular sans-serif, black #000000, about 3.5 percent of slide height. Chart title in semibold sans-serif, grey #7F7E7B, about 3.5 percent. Axis and legend labels in regular sans-serif, about 2.5 percent, grey #7F7E7B for numeric axis labels and dark grey #3F3F3F for category and legend labels. Footer text regular sans-serif, about 2.5 percent, grey #666666 for page number and #7F7E7B for source. Gridlines are fine dotted grey lines. Area shapes are flat with no outlines, no shadows, no gradients and square corners. Accent palette of warm oranges (#D89562, #CE7C3B) for digital categories against greys and slate blue-grey for traditional media.

### Slide 56

**Text**

“Your margin is my
opportunity”
- Jeff Bezos
(So what is advertising?)
55

### Slide 61

**Text**

Retail S-Curves / Walmart used new technologies to change retail – the same is happening again / Annual revenue ($bn, real terms) / Amazon / Walmart / 600 / 500 / 400 / 300 / 200 / 100 / 0 / 1980 / 1985 / 1990 / 1995 / 2000 / 2005 / 2010 / 2015 / 60 / Source: Amazon, Bloomberg

**Layout**

Full-width white slide with a light grey chart panel spanning the full width from about 24.7% to 89.1% of the height. The title and subtitle are left-aligned at x 19.1% on a common left edge, sitting in the upper white band. The chart legend sits at the far left (x 1.9% to 8%) inside the grey panel, with the chart plot area starting at x 19.3%. The footer row below the panel holds the page number at the left margin, a faint logo wordmark aligned with the chart's left edge, and the source line right-aligned to x 97.9%.

**Visuals**

Line chart titled 'Annual revenue ($bn, real terms)'. Y-axis: annual revenue in $bn, real terms, range 0 to 600, gridlines and labels at 0, 100, 200, 300, 400, 500, 600. X-axis: years 1980 to 2015, labels at 1980, 1985, 1990, 1995, 2000, 2005, 2010, 2015. Series 1, Walmart (dark grey line, about 4px wide): about 1 in 1980, about 2 in 1981, about 2 in 1983, about 10 in 1985, about 20 in 1988, about 35 in 1990, about 55 in 1992, about 95 in 1995, about 118 in 1997, about 138 in 1998, about 165 in 1999, about 195 in 2000, about 215 in 2001, about 230 in 2002, about 256 in 2003, about 287 in 2004, about 309 in 2005, about 349 in 2006, about 376 in 2007, about 404 in 2008, about 407 in 2009, about 422 in 2010, about 470 in 2012, about 476 in 2013, about 484 in 2014, about 480 in 2015. Series 2, Amazon (orange line, about 4px wide, starts 1995): about 1 in 1995, about 1 in 1997, about 4 in 1999, about 4 in 2001, about 7 in 2003, about 7 in 2004, about 10 in 2005, about 10 in 2006, about 14 in 2007, about 21 in 2008, about 24 in 2009, about 33 in 2010, about 60 in 2012, about 75 in 2013, about 89 in 2014, about 107 in 2015. Values are estimates from the plot as no values are printed. Legend at top-left of plot: orange line labelled Amazon, dark grey line labelled Walmart. No highlighted points. Gridlines are horizontal dotted grey lines at each 100 step, and the 0 baseline is a solid grey line.

**Design**

Background white #FFFFFF with a light grey chart panel #F2F2F2. Title in ProximaNova Regular, near-black #101010, about 5.9% of slide height, sans-serif, normal weight. Subtitle in ProximaNova Regular, black #000000, about 3.5% of slide height. Chart title in ProximaNova Semibold, grey #7F7E7B, about 3.5% of slide height. Axis value labels in ProximaNova Regular, grey #7F7E7B, about 2.5% of slide height. Year labels and legend labels in ProximaNova Regular, dark grey #3F3F3F, about 2.5% of slide height. Footer page number #666666 and source line #7F7E7B, about 2.5% of slide height. Series colours: Amazon orange #D07A35, Walmart dark grey #666666. Gridlines dotted grey #7F7E7B, hairline. Axis baseline solid grey #7F7E7B, hairline. Flat design, no shadows, no gradients, no rounded corners.

**Layout**

A single full-width column on a white slide. The title sits top-left at x 19.1%, aligned to the chart's left edge, with white space above and below it. A light grey panel spans the full slide width from about 24.7% to 89.1% of the height and holds the chart title, the column chart and its category labels. The footer row below the panel holds the page number at left (x 2.5%), the wordmark at x 19.1%, and the source line right-aligned to x 96.0%.

**Visuals**

Chart: vertical column chart titled 'Global revenue, 2015 ($bn)'. Y-axis: revenue in $bn, scale 0 to 1,400 with labelled ticks every 200 (0, 200, 400, 600, 800, 1,000, 1,200, 1,400). X-axis: seven sector categories, no axis title. Values are not printed on the bars; estimates from bar heights: Car sales about 1,250; Advertising about 535; Consumer Media about 420; Mobile phones about 350; Pay TV about 330; Online advertising about 150; TV production about 130. Series: single series. Colour: Car sales and Advertising are dark grey #666666; Consumer Media, Mobile phones, Pay TV, Online advertising and TV production are orange #CE7C3B. Gridlines: horizontal dotted grey lines at each 200 step. Baseline: solid grey axis line at 0. Legend: none. Highlighted points: the two grey columns are set apart from the orange ones by colour. Logo: the Andreessen Horowitz wordmark in pale grey at the bottom left, a 24 by 2 pixel image box.

**Design**

Background white #FFFFFF with a light grey #F2F2F2 band behind the chart. Title colour near-black #101010; chart title and y-axis labels and source in warm grey #7F7E7B; category labels dark grey #3F3F3F; page number #666666. Accents: orange #CE7C3B and grey #666666 for the columns. Typeface: Proxima Nova (geometric sans-serif). Title Regular at about 5.9% of slide height. Chart title Semibold at about 3.5%. Axis labels, category labels and source Regular at about 2.5%. Lines: dotted grey gridlines, solid grey baseline. Columns are flat rectangles with square corners and no shadows or gradients.

**Layout**

Single column of content on a white upper band with a full-width light grey chart panel below. The title sits top-left, left edge at about 19% of slide width, with its cap height near 7% from the top. The chart panel spans the full slide width from about 24.7% to 89% of slide height, and the chart heading sits at the panel's top-left inside the same left margin as the title. A footer row below the panel holds the page number at far left, the wordmark centred-left at about 19%, and the source line right-aligned to a margin of about 2.5%.

**Visuals**

Bar chart titled Global revenue, 2015 ($bn). Vertical axis runs from 0 to 25,000 in $bn with gridlines every 5,000 drawn as dotted grey lines; tick labels read 0, 5,000, 10,000, 15,000, 20,000 and 25,000. Horizontal category axis with a solid grey baseline. Single series, seven bars in one colour #CE7C3B, all bars with equal width and no data labels printed. Values are not printed, so estimates are: Retail about 22,000; Car sales about 1,200; Advertising about 500; Consumer Media about 400; Mobile phones about 400; Pay TV about 250; Online advertising about 100. No legend. No highlighted points; Retail is the tallest bar and is visually dominant. The wordmark ANDREESSEN HOROWITZ is a light grey logo in the footer at about 19% x and 93% y.

**Design**

Background white #FFFFFF for the upper area with a light grey #F2F2F2 chart panel across the middle. Title is in ProximaNova Regular, near-black #101010, about 5.9% of slide height in size (roughly 6% em). Chart heading is ProximaNova Semibold, grey #7F7E7B, about 3.5% em. Axis tick labels are ProximaNova Regular, grey #7F7E7B, about 2.5% em. Category labels are ProximaNova Regular, dark grey #3F3F3F, about 2.5% em. Body text is sans-serif throughout. Bars are flat #CE7C3B with no corner radius, shadow or gradient. Gridlines are dotted grey 1px lines. Baseline is a solid grey line. Page number and source line are ProximaNova Regular at about 2.5% em, colours #666666 and #7F7E7B. Wordmark is a light grey serif capital logo, about #D9D9D9 to #E6E6E6.

### Slide 65

**Text**

Cars as phones
“History doesn’t repeat, but sometimes it rhymes”
Today’s tech moat disappears.
Key components become commodities.
Value moves to software.
Network effects move away from
hardware.
‘Phone’ means something totally different.
64
ANDREESSEN HOROWITZ

**Layout**

Left-aligned title block at top with a subtitle quote beneath it, starting at about 19% of slide width. A full-width light grey band (about 25% to 89% of slide height) holds a two-column arrangement: a tilted Nokia handset photo with a grey arrow on the left, and a stacked list of five statements on the right starting at about 48% width. The white footer strip below the band carries the page number at the far left (about 2.5% from left edge) and the Andreessen Horowitz wordmark aligned with the title's left edge.

**Visuals**

One photograph of a Nokia-style candy-bar/flip-style mobile phone (dark grey-brown body with silver keypad surround, silver top bezel, grey LCD screen, black navigation pad with green call and red end keys, keypad digits 1 to 9, asterisk, 0 and hash) shown at an angle, tilted with the antenna at top and the lower-right back cover extended downward. It is a cut-out photo on the white/grey band with no box, with a soft shadow beneath. Photo is not cropped and sits over the grey band. Lighting is even and frontal, with dominant colours dark olive-grey #3B3A33 and silver #C8C8C8. Next to it is a solid grey right-pointing block arrow, shaft and triangular head, colour #A3A3A3, no outline, starting at the phone's keypad edge and pointing toward the text column. The file also contains many 1-pixel and sliver image fragments with no visible content, which are not described as elements. Logo: the Andreessen Horowitz wordmark in serif capitals in light grey #D9D9D9 at the bottom.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF, with a full-width #F2F2F2 band from 24.7% to 89% of slide height. Plain white background with a light grey #F2F2F2 horizontal band across the middle-lower slide. Title in ProximaNova Regular (geometric sans-serif, regular weight), colour #101010, cap height large, about 5.9% of slide height. Subtitle in ProximaNova Regular, #000000, about 3.5% of slide height. Body statements in ProximaNova Light (sans-serif, light weight), colour #3F3F3F, about 4.4% of slide height, with generous vertical spacing between statements. Page number in ProximaNova Regular, #666666, about 2.5% of slide height. Arrow in flat #A3A3A3 with no shadow. No gradients, no corner radii, no rules or borders.

**Visuals**

Two flat light-grey panels form the two columns, with a white chevron band between them. The chevron is a thick white stripe with a thinner grey (#BFBFBF) edge on its right side; its apex points right at about 53% of slide width and about 70% of slide height, and it cuts through the boundary between the two panels. The only other non-text element is the Andreessen Horowitz wordmark, a small light-grey serif logotype in capitals, placed in the footer. No photographs, charts or icons.

**Layout**

A single-column slide with the title and subtitle centred at the top on a white band, and a full-width light grey chart panel from about 25% to 89% of the slide height. The chart fills the panel, with its legend at the far left, left axis values beside the plot, right axis values at the right edge, and years along the bottom. The footer sits on white below the panel: slide number at the bottom left, a faint wordmark at centre left, and the source line right-aligned at the bottom right. Margins are about 2% on the left and right.

**Visuals**

Combo chart titled 'Lithium-ion battery economics' with two series sharing an x-axis of years 2008 to 2015. Left y-axis: 'Battery $/KwH', range 0 to 1,200 in steps of 200, with dotted horizontal gridlines at each step. Right y-axis: 'Energy density (watt hours / litre), RH scale', range 0 to 300 in steps of 50. Series 1, grey columns (#666666), battery $/KwH, values about: 2008 1,000; 2009 940; 2010 750; 2011 590; 2012 440; 2013 345; 2014 305; 2015 265 (no values are printed; all are estimates). Series 2, orange line (about #C87A3A), energy density on the right axis, values about: 2008 12; 2009 24; 2010 61; 2011 73; 2012 102; 2013 115; 2014 176; 2015 290 (estimates). Legend at left: grey rectangle swatch labelled 'Battery $/KwH' and orange line swatch labelled 'Energy density (watt hours / litre), RH scale'. No highlighted points. The chart is not boxed separately; it sits on the #F2F2F2 panel. Logo: a faint light grey wordmark 'ANDREESSEN HOROWITZ' in serif capitals, low contrast, about 24 by 2 px in the source image.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF across the whole slide, with a #F2F2F2 band behind the chart. White slide background with a full-width #F2F2F2 light grey band behind the chart area. Title in Proxima Nova Regular, near-black #101010, large, sentence case, left-aligned to the title block but centred on the slide. Subtitle in Proxima Nova Regular, black #000000. Chart title in Proxima Nova Semibold, grey #7F7E7B. Axis and year labels in Proxima Nova Regular, #7F7F7F on the right axis and #7F7E7B on the left, and #3F3F3F for years and legend text. Footer in Proxima Nova Regular, #666666 for the slide number and #7F7E7B for the source line. Title is about 5.9% of slide height; subtitle and body labels about 3.5% and 2.5%. Chart gridlines are dotted grey. Bars are flat with no corner radius or shadow. No gradients.

**Layout**

Full-width slide on a white background with a title block starting at about 19% from the left edge, aligned left with the chart title and the axis-label column. The title sits at the top (about 7% from the top), followed by a two-line subtitle. A full-width light grey panel spans from about 25% to 89% of the slide height and holds a horizontal bar chart with category labels on the left and a value axis along the bottom. The footer row at about 93% holds a page number at the left margin, a faint Andreessen Horowitz wordmark in the centre-left aligned with the chart's left edge, and a source line right-aligned to about 96%.

**Visuals**

Horizontal bar chart titled '2015 capex ($bn)'. Value axis along the bottom, labelled 0.0 to 15.0 in steps of 2.5 (0.0, 2.5, 5.0, 7.5, 10.0, 12.5, 15.0), no axis title, units given in the chart title as $bn. Category axis on the left lists 11 companies from top to bottom: Tesla, Nissan, Honda, BMW, Ford, GM, Daimler, Google, Toyota, Apple, VW. No values are printed on the bars; estimated values, marked as about: Tesla about 2.4, Nissan about 5.4, Honda about 5.6, BMW about 6.9, Ford about 7.7, GM about 9.0, Daimler about 9.7, Google about 11.5, Toyota about 12.9, Apple about 13.0, VW about 14.7. Highlighted bars in orange #CE7C3B: Tesla, Google, Apple. All other bars are dark grey #666666. No legend. Vertical dotted grey gridlines at each 2.5 interval. Solid grey vertical axis line at zero. Bars are thin horizontal rectangles of equal height with even spacing.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF with a light grey #F2F2F2 panel behind the chart area. Clean white slide with a light grey #F2F2F2 chart panel. Title in ProximaNova-Regular, near-black #101010, about 5.9% of slide height, sentence case. Subtitle in ProximaNova-Regular, black #000000, about 3.5% of slide height, two lines with tight leading. Accent colour orange #CE7C3B for highlighted bars, with a brighter orange #F4830A also present in the file. Bars are flat with no shadows, gradients or rounded corners. Gridlines are dotted grey. Axis line is solid grey.
